## Commutation

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000474","sourceNodeId":"n0000474","sourceUnitIds":["u000419"],"sourceAnchor":"Commutation","status":"source_ready"} -->

Commutation

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000475","sourceNodeId":"n0000475","sourceUnitIds":["u000420"],"sourceAnchor":"Commutation is an interaction between distinct non-eraser agents.","status":"source_ready"} -->

Commutation is an interaction between distinct non-eraser agents.

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000476","sourceNodeId":"n0000476","sourceUnitIds":["u000420"],"sourceAnchor":"In commutation, each agent travels through the other and may be copied according to the other agent's arity.","status":"source_ready"} -->

In commutation, each agent travels through the other and may be copied according to the other agent's arity.

## Level / delta

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000481","sourceNodeId":"n0000481","sourceUnitIds":["u000423"],"sourceAnchor":"Level and delta","status":"source_ready"} -->

Level and delta

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000482","sourceNodeId":"n0000482","sourceUnitIds":["u000424"],"sourceAnchor":"In a canonical λ encoding, entering an application's argument adds one level.","status":"source_ready"} -->

In a canonical λ encoding, entering an application's argument adds one level.

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000483","sourceNodeId":"n0000483","sourceUnitIds":["u000424"],"sourceAnchor":"Entering an application's function or an abstraction body does not add a level.","status":"source_ready"} -->

Entering an application's function or an abstraction body does not add a level.

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000484","sourceNodeId":"n0000484","sourceUnitIds":["u000424"],"sourceAnchor":"A replicator port's delta equals the bound-variable occurrence level minus the replicator level.","status":"source_ready"} -->

A replicator port's delta equals the bound-variable occurrence level minus the replicator level.

## Proper Δ-Net

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000485","sourceNodeId":"n0000485","sourceUnitIds":["u000425"],"sourceAnchor":"Proper Δ-Net","status":"source_ready"} -->

Proper Δ-Net

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000486","sourceNodeId":"n0000486","sourceUnitIds":["u000426"],"sourceAnchor":"A proper Δ-Net is reachable from a canonical Δ-Net through zero or more core Δ interactions.","status":"source_ready"} -->

A proper Δ-Net is reachable from a canonical Δ-Net through zero or more core Δ interactions.

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000487","sourceNodeId":"n0000487","sourceUnitIds":["u000426"],"sourceAnchor":"A proper Δ-Net may contain noncanonical intermediate shapes.","status":"source_ready"} -->

A proper Δ-Net may contain noncanonical intermediate shapes.

## Sharing scope

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000491","sourceNodeId":"n0000491","sourceUnitIds":["u000429"],"sourceAnchor":"Sharing scope","status":"source_ready"} -->

Sharing scope

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000492","sourceNodeId":"n0000492","sourceUnitIds":["u000430"],"sourceAnchor":"A sharing scope is the region controlled by one duplication context.","status":"source_ready"} -->

A sharing scope is the region controlled by one duplication context.

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000493","sourceNodeId":"n0000493","sourceUnitIds":["u000430"],"sourceAnchor":"Older interaction-net approaches often marked sharing-scope boundaries with separate delimiter agents.","status":"source_ready"} -->

Older interaction-net approaches often marked sharing-scope boundaries with separate delimiter agents.

## Leftmost-outermost

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000494","sourceNodeId":"n0000494","sourceUnitIds":["u000431"],"sourceAnchor":"Leftmost-outermost","status":"source_ready"} -->

Leftmost-outermost

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000495","sourceNodeId":"n0000495","sourceUnitIds":["u000432"],"sourceAnchor":"Leftmost-outermost reduction first chooses a reducible pattern that is not nested inside another reducible pattern.","status":"source_ready"} -->

Leftmost-outermost reduction first chooses a reducible pattern that is not nested inside another reducible pattern.

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000496","sourceNodeId":"n0000496","sourceUnitIds":["u000432"],"sourceAnchor":"After choosing an outermost pattern, leftmost-outermost reduction chooses the leftmost such pattern in the term-like reading of the net.","status":"source_ready"} -->

After choosing an outermost pattern, leftmost-outermost reduction chooses the leftmost such pattern in the term-like reading of the net.

## Primary source

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000497","sourceNodeId":"n0000497","sourceUnitIds":["u000433"],"sourceAnchor":"Primary source","status":"source_ready"} -->

Primary source

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000498","sourceNodeId":"n0000498","sourceUnitIds":["u000434"],"sourceAnchor":"The primary source is Daniel Augusto Rizzi Salvadori's paper “Δ-Nets: Interaction-Based System for Optimal Parallel λ-Reduction,” arXiv:2505.20314v4, dated 22 July 2026.","status":"source_ready"} -->

The primary source is Daniel Augusto Rizzi Salvadori's paper “Δ-Nets: Interaction-Based System for Optimal Parallel λ-Reduction,” arXiv:2505.20314v4, dated 22 July 2026.

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000499","sourceNodeId":"n0000499","sourceUnitIds":["u000435"],"sourceAnchor":"The HTML version of the primary source is available on arXiv.","status":"source_ready"} -->

The HTML version of the primary source is available on arXiv.

The arXiv abstract and downloadable formats are available online.

<!-- refine:source {"id":"teach-n0000501","sourceNodeId":"n0000501","sourceUnitIds":["u000437"],"sourceAnchor":"This edition is an independently structured teaching summary of the paper's abstract and Sections 1–5.","status":"source_ready"} -->
